def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha):
  c = corr_counts(x)
  m = c.max()
  shifted = alpha*(c-m)
  # LSE = m + log(sum exp(shifted))/alpha
  # If sum underflow? max 0 so sum >=1.
  return float(m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))/alpha)

def smooth_obj_grad(x,n,alpha):
  c=corr_counts(x)
  m=c.max()
  shifted=alpha*(c-m)
  W=np.exp(shifted); Wsum=W.sum(); W/=Wsum
  f = (m + np.log(Wsum)/alpha) * (2.0/n)
  grad_counts = conv... (W)
  grad = grad_counts * (2.0/n)
  return f, grad

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)

    h0=project_feasible(parent,n0) if len matches else interp to n0? If len(n_par) maybe odd choose even.
    evaluate, set best.

      store starts list (n,hv,c) maybe top 3
  if best_h None: create random? n=250 full 0.5 random.

def alm_wrapper(x, n, alpha, lam, mu):
    f, grad = smooth_once(x,n,alpha)
    comp = x.sum() - n/2
    val = f + lam*comp + 0.5*mu*comp*comp
    g = grad
    if (lam!=0 or mu!=0):
       g = grad + (lam + mu*comp)*np.ones_like(x)  # alloc
    return val,g

smooth_once implementation:
```
def smooth_once(x,n,alpha):
  c = corr_counts(x)
  m = float(c.max())
  shifted = alpha*(c - m)
  ex = np.exp(shifted)
  s = ex.sum()
  W = ex/s
  f = (m + np.log(s)/alpha) * (2.0/n)
  grad = grad_weights(x,W,n) * (2.0/n)
  return f, grad
```
If alpha=0? not.
Potential overflow: shifted max 0, min could be -alpha*diff. If diff counts ~n (500), alpha 10000 => -5e6, exp underflow to 0; okay. But if c all equal, s=L, log. Good.

Let's test for d=0 only: W[off]=1. conv(1-h,W) length L+n-1; slice off:off+n should equal 1-h. h*W[::-1] slice: W[::-1][off]? W reversed has 1 at off. conv yields h slice. grad=1-2h. Good.

Implement:
```
def pair_delta_count(h, off, i, j, d):
    L=2*off+1
    delta=np.zeros(L)
    o=1-h
    hr=h[::-1]
    delta[off-i : off+n-i] += d*o
    delta[i : i+off+1] += -d*hr  # length off+1=n? off+1=n. i+off+1 <=2n-1? i<=n-1 => i+n <=2n-1 =L, good.
    delta[off-j : off+n-j] += -d*o
    delta[j : j+n] += d*hr
    delta[off] += -2*d*d
    if i != j:
       delta[off + (j - i)] += d*d
       delta[off + (i - j)] += d*d
    return delta
```
Wait second slice length n: i:i+n, yes not off+1? off+1=n. Good.
Test n=2 i0 j1 d: o=[1-h0,1-h1], hr=[h1,h0]
i first [off-i=1:1+2=3] +d o: idx1 d o0? q? p=off+b-i: b=0 idx1 s=0? Actually c_shift index off+s, for b=0 s=-1 idx0? Wait first idx=off+b-i: i=0,b=0 idx=1 (s=0, self) but b=0 not i? def local_search(h,n,deadline,rng, max_iter=None, anneal=False):
  h=project; c=corr_counts(h)[::-1]; best_m=c.max(); best_c=best_m*2/n
  temp=np.zeros(2*n-1); cand=np.empty_like(c)
  deltas_sizes=[0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002] maybe scaled sqrt? 
  # We can cycle deltas; for each, random pairs, accept improvements.
  # number iterations determined by time. Use maybe 200k max. 
  for it in range(...):
    if time > deadline: break
    d = rng.uniform(0.0005,0.03)
    sign = 1 if rng.rand()<0.5 else -1
    i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
    if i==j continue
    h_i=h[i]; h_j=h[j]
    nd=h_i+d*sign; ... bounds
    temp compute; newm = (c+temp).max()
    if newm < best_m - 1e-12:
       c+=temp; h[i]=nd; h[j]=...; best_m=newm; best_c=best_m*2/n; update best_h copy? copying full each accept expensive but okay if many; we can store best_h = h.copy() on accept. Or update at end.
    else reject.
  final recompute c exact for best_h; return.
```

def active_gradient_step(h,n,dx):

```
def local_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_eval=200000, anneal=True):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    c = corr_counts(h)[::-1]
    cur_m = float(c.max())
    best_m = cur_m
    best_h = h.copy()
    off=n-1; L=2*off+1
    temp=np.zeros(L, dtype=float); cand=np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    # Temperature schedule
    n_iter = 0
    # estimate eval time? We'll loop until deadline or max_eval
    T0 = max(0.05, 0.004*n)  # counts ~0.25? target 0.19n; T0 1% n? 0.004n -> C 0.008.
    Tend = 0.0002*n if n>10 else 1e-4
    # number iterations maybe 100000
    total_iters = 200000
    for it in range(total_iters):
        if time.time() > deadline or it>=total_iters: break
        T = T0 * (Tend/T0)**(it/total_iters)
        i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n)
        if i==j: continue
        # choose delta magnitude adaptive: maybe based on distance to bounds
        # Sample from log range [1e-4, 0.05]
        d = rng.uniform(1e-4, 0.05)
        if rng.rand() < 0.5: d = -d
        hi=h[i]+d; hj=h[j]-d
        if hi < 0 or hi > 1 or hj <0 or hj>1: continue
        # compute delta
        temp.fill(0.0)
        om = 1.0 - h
        hr = h[::-1]
        temp[off-i:off+n-i] += d*om
        temp[i:i+n] += -d*hr
        temp[off-j:off+n-j] -= d*om
        temp[j:j+n] += d*hr
        temp[off] += -2.0*d*d
        if i != j:
           temp[off + j - i] += d*d
           temp[off + i - j] += d*d
        np.add(c, temp, out=cand)
        new_m = float(cand.max())
        delta_m = new_m - cur_m
        accept = False
        if new_m <= cur_m + 1e-15:
           accept = new_m < best_m - 1e-13 or rng.rand() < 0.2? no
        else:
           if T>0 and np.log(rng.rand()) < -delta_m/T:
              accept = True
        if accept:
           h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
           c += temp
           cur_m = new_m
           if new_m < best_m - 1e-13:
              best_m = new_m; best_h = h.copy()
           if delta_m <=0:
              # if improvement, maybe reduce T? optional
              pass
        # optional recompute every 5000 accepts to correct drift
    # Final project/recompute
    h_best = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    c5 = evaluate(h_best)
    return h_best,c5
```

Let's implement `active_polish(h,n,deadline,rng)` using single-lag gradient steps before random local. This can make continuous improvements.
```
def single_lag_grad_out(h,n,p):
  off=n-1; s=off-p
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  if s>=0:
    a=np.arange(n-s)
    grad[a] += 1-h[a+s]
    grad[a+s] -= h[a]
  else:
    q=-s
    a=np.arange(q,n)
    grad[a] += 1-h[a-q]
    grad[a-q] -= h[a]
  return grad
```
Note for c_out order p, s=off-p (positive at low p). Works for d=0: a=np.arange(n); grad +=1-h - h =1-2h.
Use top k lags: c_out=corr_counts(h); m=max. weights maybe exp(alpha*(c-m)) for top 5. grad=sum weights*single_lag_grad. Or for exact max subgradient use average of max lags. Use projection.

def active_polish(h,n,deadline,steps=5000):

    time check

    # h currently feasible. grad scaled? Try lrs [1/n,0.5/n,0.2/n,0.05/n]

       hp=project(h - lr*grad)
       cn=corr(hp); mn=cn.max()

    if no accept: break or perturb
```

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from scipy.signal import fftconvolve

def corr_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def c5_from_counts(c,n):
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0/n))

def project_feasible(v,n):
   target = 0.5*n
   v = np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
   # If v shape mismatch? clip
   v = np.clip(v, 0,1)?? Wait projection with τ should start from arbitrary, clipping in bisect. If we preclip, okay but changes projection. Use v copy.
   lo = -np.max(v) - 1.0
   hi = 1.0 - np.min(v)
   for _ in range(80): ...
   x=np.clip(v+tau,0,1)
   # correct sum
   diff = target - x.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       if diff>0:
          mask=x<1.0; if mask.any(): x[mask] += diff/np.count_nonzero(mask)
       else:
          mask=x>0.0; if mask.any(): x[mask] += diff/np.count_nonzero(mask)
       x=np.clip(x,0,1)
   return x
```

- `smooth_once(x,n,alpha)`:
```
def smooth_once(x,n,alpha):
    c=corr_counts(x)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    shifted = alpha*(c-m)
    ex=np.exp(shifted)
    s=ex.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: # fallback one-hot
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); s=1; ex=np.zeros_like(c); ex[idx]=1.0
    W=ex/s
    f=(m + np.log(s)/alpha)*(2.0/n)
    # gradient
    L=2*n-1; off=n-1
    g=1.0-x
    if n <= 700:
       c1=np.convolve(g, W, mode='full')
       c2=np.convolve(x, W[::-1], mode='full')
    else:
       c1=fftconvolve(g, W, mode='full')
       c2=fftconvolve(x, W[::-1], mode='full')
    grad=(c1[off:off+n] - c2[off:off+n])*(2.0/n)
    return f, grad
```
Need ensure W[::-1] contour? For n small, W dtype float. fftconvolve may return length L? It pads; okay. For n <=700 use np.convolve, which may be faster than fft for small. For n=1000 maybe fft. If fftconvolve not imported? It is. If not available, fallback? Could wrap try.

- `alm_fun(x,n,alpha,lam,mu)`:
```
def alm_fun(x,n,alpha,lam,mu):
   f, grad = smooth_once(x,n,alpha)
   comp = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*n)
   val = f + lam*comp + 0.5*mu*comp*comp
   if lam !=0.0 or mu !=0.0:
       grad = grad + (lam + mu*comp)
   return val, float?
```
L-BFGS-B returns (fun, jac). Good. It expects finite. If val nan, return large.
Potential: adding scalar to grad array returns new; okay.

def run_lm(h0,n,alpha,lam,mu,maxiter):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res = minimize(alm_fun, h0, args=(n,alpha,lam,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'maxls':30})
       if res is None: return h0.copy()
       x=res.x
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return h0.copy()
       return project_feasible(x,n)
    except Exception:
       return h0.copy()
```

- `single_lag_grad(h,n,p)`: p out index.
```
def single_lag_grad(h,n,p):
    off=n-1; s=off-p
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    if s>=0:
       m=n-s
       if m>0:
          a=np.arange(m)
          grad[a] += 1.0 - h[a+s]
          grad[a+s] -= h[a]
    else:
       q=-s
       m=n-q
       if m>0:
          a=np.arange(q,n)
          grad[a] += 1.0 - h[a-q]
          grad[a-q] -= h[a]
    return grad
```
For s=0, a=np.arange(n); grad[a]+=1-h[a]; grad[a]-=h[a] =>1-2h. Good.

- `active_polish(h,n,deadline,max_steps=2000, tol=1e-12)`:
```
def active_polish(h,n,deadline,max_steps=2000):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
    c=corr_counts(h)
    off=n-1
    best_m=float(c.max()); best_c=best_m*2.0/n; best_h=h.copy()
    no_improve=0
    for step in range(max_steps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # active weights top 8
       active_idx = np.argsort(c)[-8:]
       # weights softmax with alpha maybe 100
       maxc = c[active_idx].max()
       w = np.exp(100*(c[active_idx]-maxc))
       w /= w.sum()
       grad = np.zeros(n)
       for p,wt in zip(active_idx,w):
          if wt > 1e-8:
             grad += wt * single_lag_grad(h,n,int(p))
       # try lrs
       accepted=False
       # base lr 2/n down
       base = 2.0/n
       for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625]:
           hp = project_feasible(h - base*mult*grad, n)
           cn = corr_counts(hp)
           mn = float(cn.max())
           if mn < best_m - 1e-12:
               h=hp; c=cn; best_m=mn; best_c=mn*2.0/n; best_h=h.copy(); accepted=True; no_improve=0; break
       if not accepted:
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 100: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
This can be heavy if each step tries multiple lr and correlate. max steps maybe 500. We'll cap by time. For n=600, each correlate 360k; 500*3=1M*? fine. But if no accept quickly stops.
Maybe choose top 3 not 8 to reduce. We'll set top=5.

- `local_search(h,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=300000)`:
Implement annealing/hill as above. Need ensure not too slow. Use max_iters but time break. For n large, reduce.
```
def local_search(h,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=300000):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
    c=corr_counts(h)[::-1]  # shift order
    off=n-1; L=2*off+1
    cur_m=float(c.max()); best_m=cur_m; best_h=h.copy()
    temp=np.zeros(L); cand=np.empty(L)
    om=1.0-h
    hr=h[::-1]
    T0=max(0.02, 0.002*n) # maybe small
    Tend=0.00005*n
    # If n small T0 maybe 0.5? For n=250, T0=0.5 count (C 0.004)
    # total iters based on n: max(50000, int(20000000/n))? 
    total = int(max(50000, min(max_iters, 20000000 / max(1,n))))
    # Evaluate roughly? We'll loop.
    for it in range(total):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       T = T0 * (Tend/T0)**(it/total)
       i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n)
       if i==j: continue
       # choose d log uniform? 
       d = 10.0**rng.uniform(-4, np.log10(0.05)) # 1e-4 to 0.05
       if rng.rand()<0.5: d=-d
       hi=h[i]+d; hj=h[j]-d
       if hi<0.0 or hi>1.0 or hj<0.0 or hj>1.0: 
           continue
       temp.fill(0.0)
       # slices
       # Note om length n; hr view length n
       a0=off-i; a1=off+n-i
       temp[a0:a1] += d * om
       b0=i; b1=i+n
       temp[b0:b1] += -d * hr
       a0=off-j; a1=off+n-j
       temp[a0:a1] -= d * om
       b0=j; b1=j+n
       temp[b0:b1] += d * hr
       temp[off] += -2.0*d*d
       if i != j:
          temp[off + j - i] += d*d
          temp[off + i - j] += d*d
       np.add(c, temp, out=cand)
       new_m=float(cand.max())
       diff=new_m-cur_m
       if new_m < best_m - 1e-13:
           # accept improvement always (also update best)
           h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj; c += temp; cur_m=new_m; best_m=new_m; best_h=h.copy(); om[i]=1-hi; om[j]=1-hj
       elif diff <= 1e-13:
           # maybe accept small equal with prob
           if rng.rand()<0.1:
              h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj; c += temp; cur_m=new_m; om[i]=1-hi; om[j]=1-hj
       else:
           if T>0 and np.log(rng.rand()) < -diff/T:
              h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj; c += temp; cur_m=new_m; om[i]=1-hi; om[j]=1-hj
    # final
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    bc=c5_from_counts(corr_counts(best_h),n)
    # also check current h if better
    hc=c5_from_counts(corr_counts(h),n)
    if hc<bc: best_h=h; bc=hc
    return best_h,bc
```
Need be careful: `om` after reject h unchanged. If accept, om[i], om[j] update. `hr` view h[::-1] updates. Good.
But in the slices, `temp[...] += d * om` uses `om` values for all positions including i,j before change. That's correct for linear delta. Cross corrections account product. Good.
For `temp[...]` with rn, if slices overlap, numpy handles sequential adding. For candidate, we don't modify h until accept.
Recompute current h vs best_h at end.

But accepting too much may wander; total cooling. We can set T0=0.002*n to be safer. Parent near target; small improvements needed. Use T0=0.003*n, Tend=0.00005*n. For n=600 T0=1.8, Tend=0.03.
Number total iterations: If n=250, 20M/250=80k; okay. n=600=33k. Maybe enough. We can allocate remaining time, e.g. 300s, with 80k evals each eval O(n) slices. Should finish. Might be underuse; can increase: `total = min(max_iters, max(20000, int(100000000 / n)))`? 100M/n for 250=400k. Each eval maybe 10us? 400k=4s? but Python maybe 0.1ms =>40s. Could be okay. def optimize_start(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h0.copy(),n)
    c5=evaluate; best_h=h; best_c=c5
    # ALM
    alphas=[20,100,500,2000]
    mu=100.0; lam=0.0
    for ai in range(len(alphas)):
       a=alphas[ai]
       # 2 stages
       for stage in range(2):
           time_left=deadline-time.time()
           if time_left<10: break
           maxiter=80 if n<=300 else 50 if n<=600 else 30
           # if stage 0 maybe maxiter 120? 
           h_new = run_lm(h,n,a,lam,mu,maxiter)
           c_new=evaluate(h_new)
           if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
               best_c=c_new; best_h=h_new.copy()
           # choose next current: if c_new <= best_c*1.01? maybe use h_new if feasible and not much worse
           # compute exact for h_new; use if <= current c? keep h? We can track current_c.
           # For next penalty update use h_new
           comp = h_new.sum() - 0.5*n
           lam = lam + mu * comp
           if abs(comp) > 1e-6:
               mu = min(mu*3.0, 1e7)
           else:
               mu = min(mu*1.5, 1e6)
           h = h_new
       # if no improvement maybe perturb? 
    # active polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 30:
       hp, cp = active_polish(h,n,deadline - 30, max_steps=300)
       if cp < best_c: update
    # local search maybe handled in run separately, not inside? We can call here if time.
    return best_h,best_c
```

def unique_top_starts(starts, K=4):
    selected=[]
    seen_n=[]
    for c,n,h in starts:
       if n in seen_n: continue
       selected.append((c,n,h))
       if len(selected)>=K: break
    return selected
```

```
def enforce_sum(x,n):
  target=n/2.0
  s=x.sum()
  diff=target-s
  if diff!=0:
     # adjust all elements not at bounds by diff/n
     mask=(x<1)&(x>0)
     if mask.any():
        x[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
     else:
        idx=int(np.argmax((1-x) if diff>0 else x))
        x[idx]+=diff
     x=np.clip(x,0,1)
  # one more correction by distributing tiny into random/first element
  for _ in range(3):
     s=x.sum(); diff=target-s
     if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
     mask=(x<1.0-1e-15) if diff>0 else (x>1e-15)
     if mask.any(): x[mask] += diff/mask.sum()
     else: x[0]+=diff
     x=np.clip(x,0,1)
  return x
```

F=Σ_p W_p c_p = Σ_{i,j} W_{off-(j-i)} h_i g_j. Let A[p]=W_p. T1 (first h_i): for fixed k, sum_j A[off-(j-k)]g_j = Σ_j g_j A[off+k-j]. Let Bpos = A (indexed p). Conv(g,Bpos)[off+k] = Σ_j g[j] Bpos[off+k-j] = A[off+k-j]. Good.
T2 (second h_j): sum_i A[off-(i-k)]h_i = Σ_i h_i A[off+i-k]. Need conv(h, A_rev)[off+k] = Σ_i h_i A_rev[off+k-i]. A_rev[p]=A[off-(p-off)] = A[2off-p]. At p=off+k-i: A[2off-off-k+i] = A[off - k + i] = A[off+i-k]. Good. So grad=T1-T2. Perfect.
Thus `np.convolve(1-h, W, 'full')[off:off+n] - np.convolve(h, W[::-1], 'full')[off:off+n]`.
For LSE gradient W sum =1. Good.

def optimize_start(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h0.copy(),n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_from_counts(corr_counts(h),n)
    maxiter_by_n = 120 if n<=300 else 80 if n<=500 else 50
    alphas=[10,50,200,1000,4000]
    mu=10.0; lam=0.0
    for a in alphas:
       for stage in range(2):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # choose maxiter based on remaining time? maybe if remaining<50 break
       h2=run_lm(h,n,a,lam,mu,maxiter_by_n)
       c2=eval(h2)
       if c2<best_c: update
       # always set h = h2 if finite? maybe if c2 < best_c + 0.005? else keep h0? To allow escape, set h=h2 (projected) even if slightly worse; but active next may start worse. We can decide:
       if c2 <= best_c + 0.002:
           h=h2
       else:
           h=best_h
       comp = h.sum()-0.5*n
       lam += mu*comp
       mu = min(mu*2.0, 1e4)
       # if comp small and a==last? break? 
    # active polish with current h and best_h? Use h if not much worse else best_h
    start_active = h if c5_from_counts(corr_counts(h),n) <= best_c + 0.002 else best_h
    if time.time()<deadline-20:
       ha,ca=active_polish(start_active,n,deadline-10,max_steps=400)
       if ca<best_c: update
    # local maybe not here
    return best_h,best_c

def safe_convolve(a,b):
    if fftconvolve is not None and (len(a)+len(b)) > 1200:
        try:
            return np.asarray(fftconvolve(a,b,mode='full'), dtype=float)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return np.convolve(a,b,mode='full')

```
def finalize_sum(x,n):
   target = 0.5*n
   for _ in range(5):
      s=x.sum()
      diff=target-s
      if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
      if diff > 0:
         mask = x < 1.0
         cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
         if cnt:
            x[mask] += diff / cnt
      else:
         mask = x > 0.0
         cnt=...
         if cnt: x[mask] += diff/cnt
      x=np.clip(x,0,1)
   return x
```

def evaluate_c5(h,n):

Build parents:
```
def build_parent_candidates(parent, deadline):
    res=[]
    np_len=len(parent)
    if np_len <=0: return res
    n_parent = np_len if np_len %2 ==0 else np_len+1
    base = n_parent
    deltas=[0,-20,-40,20,40,80,150,250,350,500]
    ns=[]
    for d in deltas:
       n=base+d
       if n>=100 and n<=800 and n%2==0: ns.append(n)
    for factor in [1.25,1.5]:
       n=int(round(base*factor))
       if n%2: n+=1
       if 100<=n<=800: ns.append(n)
    x_old=(np.arange(np_len)+0.5)*(2.0/np_len)
    seen=set(); cands=[]
    for n in ns:
       if n in seen: continue
       seen.add(n)
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if n==np_len:
          hv=parent.copy()
       else:
          x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
          hv=np.interp(x_new,x_old,parent)
       hv=project_feasible(hv,n)
       c=evaluate_c5(hv,n)
       res.append((c,n,hv))
    # also include n_parent if not? deltas 0 ensures if base even; if parent odd base odd? n_parent even; x_old len parent odd; interp. Good.
    return res
```

Select unique top:
```
def select_unique_starts(starts, K):
    selected=[]; seen=[]
    for item in starts:
       n=item[1]
       if n in seen: continue
       selected.append(item)
       seen.append(n)
       if len(selected)>=K: break
    return selected
```

   if time_left < 40: return 20
   if n <= 300:

   elif n <= 500:
      base=120
   elif n <= 700:
      base=80
   else:
      base=50
   if time_left < 120: base=int(base*0.5)
   if time_left < 60: base=int(base*0.25)

```
def safe_convolve(a,b):
    n=max(len(a),len(b))
    if fftconvolve is not None and n > 700:
       try: return np.asarray(fftconvolve(a,b,mode='full'), dtype=float)
       except: pass
    return np.convolve(a,b,mode='full')
```

`alm_fun`: If `mu` large, `0.5*mu*comp*comp` may overflow? comp ≤n, mu≤5e4, n=800 ->1.6e10 okay. If x nan, return large.
```
def alm_fun(x,n,alpha,lam,mu):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    f,grad=smooth_once(x,n,alpha)
    if not np.isfinite(f) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    comp = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*n)
    val = f + lam*comp + 0.5*mu*comp*comp
    if val != val or val > 1e90:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    if lam != 0.0 or mu != 0.0:
       grad = grad + (lam + mu*comp)
    return float(val), grad
```
Returning zero gradient if bad may harm but finite.

`run_lm`: pass `bounds` as list each call. Maybe `[(0.0,1.0)]*n`. If n large, okay.
```
def run_lm(h0,n,alpha,lam,mu,maxiter):
    if maxiter <=0: return h0.copy(),0.0
    try:
       res=minimize(alm_fun,h0,args=(n,alpha,lam,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n,options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'maxls':30})
       x=res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else h0
       if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return h0.copy(),0.0
       comp=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
       hp=project_feasible(x,n)
       return hp, comp
    except Exception:
       return h0.copy(), 0.0
```

`active_polish`: if `deadline` passed maybe int. In line search, if `grad` all zero break. Use `np.linalg.norm`? Could skip. If norm tiny, break.
```
def active_polish(h,n,deadline,max_steps=300):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
    c=corr_counts(h)
    off=n-1
    best_m=float(c.max()); best_c=best_m*(2.0/n); best_h=h.copy()
    no=0
    for st in range(max_steps):
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       active = np.argsort(c)[-4:]
       maxc = c[active].max()
       w = np.exp(30.0*(c[active]-maxc))
       if w.sum() <=0: w=np.ones_like(w)/len(w)
       else: w=w/w.sum()
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       for idx,wt in zip(active,w):
          if wt > 1e-10:
             grad = grad + wt * single_lag_grad(h,n,int(idx))
       gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       grad = grad / gnorm? If normalize, step size basis changes. Better not normalize; use raw scale. Use gnorm to choose base: base=1.0/(n*max(1.0,gnorm/(n)))? Simpler raw.
       base=1.0/n
       accepted=False
       for mult in (5.0,2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
          hp=project_feasible(h - base*mult*grad,n)
          cn=corr_counts(np.asarray(hp))
          mn=float(cn.max())
          if mn < best_m - 1e-12:
             h=hp; c=cn; best_m=mn; best_c=mn*(2.0/n); best_h=h.copy(); no=0; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          no +=1
          if no > 80: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
Maybe if grad raw entries O(1), base*5 ~5/n. Good.
`np.linalg.norm` O(n); okay.

`local_search`: Need T0/Tend if scales zero. If total=0? At least.
```
def local_search(h,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=200000,T0_scale=0.003,Tend_scale=0.00005):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
    c=corr_counts(h)[::-1]
    off=n-1; L=2*off+1
    cur_m=float(c.max()); best_m=cur_m; best_h=h.copy()
    temp=np.zeros(L); cand=np.empty(L)
    om=1.0-h
    hr=h[::-1]
    total=int(min(max_iters, max(20000, int(50000000/max(1,n)))))
    T0=max(1e-6, T0_scale*n)
    Tend=max(1e-8, Tend_scale*n)
    pmax=int(np.argmax(c))
    accept_count=0
    for it in range(total):
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      T = T0 * (Tend/T0)**(float(it)/float(total))
      if it % 50 == 0:
          pmax=int(np.argmax(c))
      d=0.0; i=0; j=0
      # choose move
      if rng.random() < 0.7 and pmax != off:
         s = int(pmax - off)
         if s >=0:
             a_start=0; a_stop=n-s
         else:
             a_start=-s; a_stop=n
         if a_stop > a_start:
             a=int(rng.randint(a_start,a_stop))
             b=a+s
             # move from source a to target b: h[b]+=d, h[a]-=d
             i=int(b); j=int(a)
             d=rng.uniform(1e-4,0.02)
              # if d random positive. Could also small chance opposite? Focused improving should source->target; opposite may harm. Keep positive.
         else:
             i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
             if i==j: continue
             d=rng.uniform(1e-4,0.05)
             if rng.random()<0.5: d=-d
      else:
         i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
         if i==j: continue
         d=rng.uniform(1e-4,0.05)
         if rng.random()<0.5: d=-d
      hi=h[i]+d; hj=h[j]-d
      if hi < 0.0 or hi > 1.0 or hj < 0.0 or hj > 1.0:
          continue
      temp.fill(0.0)
      temp[off-int(i):off+n-int(i)] += d * om
      temp[int(i):int(i)+n] += -d * hr
      temp[off-int(j):off+n-int(j)] -= d * om
      temp[int(j):int(j)+n] += d * hr
      temp[off] += -2.0*d*d
      di=int(i); dj=int(j)
      if di != dj:
         temp[off + dj - di] += d*d
         temp[off + di - dj] += d*d
      np.add(c, temp, out=cand)
      new_m=float(cand.max())
      diff=new_m-cur_m
      accept=False
      if new_m < best_m - 1e-13:
          accept=True; improve=True
      elif diff <= 1e-13:
          accept = (rng.random() < 0.2)
      else:
          if T > 0.0:
              # avoid log of zero
              r=rng.random()
              if r > 0.0 and math.log(r) < -diff/T:
                  accept=True
      if accept:
          h[di]=hi; h[dj]=hj
          c += temp
          cur_m=new_m
          om[di]=1.0-hi; om[dj]=1.0-hj
          accept_count +=1
          if accept_count % 5000 == 0:
              c=corr_counts(h)[::-1]
              cur_m=float(c.max())
              if cur_m < best_m - 1e-13:
                  best_m=cur_m; best_h=h.copy()
              om=1.0-h
              pmax=int(np.argmax(c))
          if new_m < best_m - 1e-13:
              best_m=new_m; best_h=h.copy()
      # maybe if current_m much larger than best and T low, reset to best? 
      if T < 1e-6 and cur_m > best_m + max(1.0, abs(best_m))*1e-3:
          # optionally revert to best to stay near
          # Do not copy every time; if diff big
          pass
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    bc=evaluate_c5(best_h,n)
    hc=evaluate_c5(h,n)
    if hc < bc:
       return h,bc? Wait if hc<bc, need return h and hc.
    else return best_h,bc
```

Now, `build_parent_candidates`: Need if parent length huge and n candidates small, interpolation. Use `np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent)`. If parent has NaN? no.

```
def build_parent_candidates(parent, deadline):
    res=[]
    m=len(parent)
    if m <= 1: return res
    base = m if (m %2==0) else m+1
    if base < 100: base=100
    if base %2: base+=1
    deltas=[0, -20, 20, -40, 40, 80, -80, 150, 250, 350, 500]
    ns=[]
    for d in deltas:
       n=base+d
       if n>=120 and n<=800 and n%2==0 and n not in ns: ns.append(n)
    for factor in [1.25,1.5,2.0]:
       n=int(round(base*factor))
       if n%2: n+=1
       if n<120: n=120
       if n>800: n=800
       if n%2: n-=1
       if n not in ns: ns.append(n)
    x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*(2.0/m)
    used=set()
    for n in ns:
       if n in used: continue
       used.add(n)
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       if n == m:
           hv=parent.astype(float, copy=True)
       else:
           x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
           hv=np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent.astype(float, copy=False))
       hv=project_feasible(hv,n)
       c=evaluate_c5(hv,n)
       if np.isfinite(c):
           res.append((c,n,hv))
    if not res:
       n=base if base%2==0 else base+1
       hv=project_feasible(parent.astype(float,copy=True), n) if len(parent)==n else project_feasible(np.full(base,0.5),base)
       c=evaluate_c5(hv,base)
       res.append((c,base,hv))
    return res
```
If parent len even =250, base=250, n=250 included hv=parent copy. Good.
If parent len odd=251, base=252; no n==m, interpolation to 252.
If parent has n odd and we want direct n odd? skip to even.

`select_unique_starts`: start items (c,n,h). Use list seen.
```
def select_unique_starts(starts, K):
    res=[]; seen=[]
    for item in starts:
       n=item[1]
       if n in seen: continue
       seen.append(n); res.append(item)
       if len(res)>=K: break
    return res
```
No lambda.

`run` final:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       parent = None
    starts=[]
    cand_deadline = t0 + min(80.0, max(20.0, budget_s*0.08))
    if parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
       starts = build_parent_candidates(parent, cand_deadline)
    if not starts:
       for n in (200, 300, 500):
           if n%2: n+=1
           hv = project_feasible(np.full(n,0.5),n)
           c = evaluate_c5(hv,n)
           starts.append((c,n,hv))
    starts.sort(key=start_key)
    selected = select_unique_starts(starts, 3)
    if not selected:
       n=250; hv=project_feasible(np.full(n,0.5),n); c=evaluate_c5(hv,n); selected=[(c,n,hv)]
    best_c=float(selected[0][0]); best_h=selected[0][2].copy(); best_n=selected[0][1]
    # local reserve
    if budget_s > 600:
       reserve = max(120.0, min(350.0, budget_s*0.3))
    else:
       reserve = max(30.0, budget_s*0.25)
    opt_deadline = deadline - reserve
    if opt_deadline < t0 + 30:
       opt_deadline = deadline - 20
    # allocate
    fracs = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]
    total_opt = opt_deadline - t0
    if total_opt > 0:
       for idx,item in enumerate(selected):
           if time.time() >= opt_deadline: break
           c,n,h = item
           frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.1
           alloc = total_opt * frac
           if alloc < 15:
              alloc = (opt_deadline - time.time()) * 0.2
           alloc_deadline = time.time() + alloc
           if alloc_deadline > opt_deadline: alloc_deadline=opt_deadline
           if time.time() >= alloc_deadline: continue
           h_opt,c_opt = optimize_start(h,n,alloc_deadline,rng)
           if c_opt < best_c:
              best_c=c_opt; best_h=h_opt.copy(); best_n=n
           total_opt = opt_deadline - time.time()
    # local polish best
    local_deadline = deadline - 3.0
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < local_deadline - 10:
        h_l,c_l = local_search(best_h,best_n,local_deadline,rng,max_iters=300000,T0_scale=0.003,Tend_scale=0.00005)
        if c_l < best_c:
            best_c=c_l; best_h=h_l.copy(); best_n=len(h_l)
        # if close second start, local
        if len(selected)>1 and time.time() < local_deadline - 60:
            c2,n2,h2 = selected[1]
            if c2 < best_c + 0.002:
                h2l,c2l = local_search(h2,n2,local_deadline - 30,rng,max_iters=150000,T0_scale=0.002,Tend_scale=0.00003)
                if c2l < best_c:
                    best_c=c2l; best_h=h2l.copy(); best_n=n2
    # final ensure
    if best_h is None:
       best_n=250; best_h=project_feasible(np.full(best_n,0.5),best_n); best_c=evaluate_c5(best_h,best_n)
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    final_c = evaluate_c5(best_h,best_n)
    if final_c > best_c + 1e-12:
        # maybe if final projection worsened, try active or simply return best_h? constraint. Can't return unprojected. Keep projected.
        pass
    return best_h, float(final_c), int(best_n)
```

Let's think about correctness of ALM with `alm_fun`: It uses smooth f scaled by dx. But L-E f uses counts m + logshift/alpha. The gradient from `smooth_once` is gradient of the same counts expression times dx. Good. Wait derivative of f = (m + log(s)/alpha) * dx /? dx=2/n constant. Gradient = (gradient of (m + log...)) * dx. We computed grad = W * grad_counts * dx. Derivative of LSE = W grad_counts. So correct.

But note f includes m. The derivative of m part is W (softmax) not just? For LSE as m + (1/α)logΣexp(α(c-m)), derivative is Σ softmax grad c, no separate m derivative because m's argmax dependency canceled. W correct. Good.

Now, `smooth_once` computes `W = exp(shifted)/s`, where shifted=α(c-m). If `s` is already normalized? f uses log(s_unscaled)/α. Gradient uses W normalized. Good.

Now, in ALM, we minimize f_smooth (C units) plus penalty. Good.

Potential issue: At high alpha, f_smooth approximates one max lag but if multiple maxima equally, W uniform over all near max. Good.

Now, does `np.exp(shifted)` for shifted much negative (e.g., -1e5) underflow all except max; s=1. Good.

Now, if n large and safe_convolve uses fftconvolve, result may have tiny imaginary? fftconvolve returns real if inputs real. good.

Let's run through small example for gradient with W one-hot positive lag. n=2, Wout[0]=1. `grad = conv(1-h,W) - conv(h,W[::-1])`. W=[1,0,0], rev=[0,0,1]. off=1. T1 slice off: [conv index1,2]. conv(g,W): index1 = g1*W0 = g1, index2= h? g0? Actually conv length3: [g0, g1, 0]? For g=[g0,g1], W=[1,0,0]: conv [g0,g1,0]. slice off=1..2 => [g1,0]. T2 conv(h,Wrev): Wrev [0,0,1], conv [0,0,h0,h1]? length4? Wait length n+L-1 =4? n=2, L=3 length 4? np.convolve length 4. slice off=1: [0, h0?]. Let's compute: h=[h0,h1], Wrev=[0,0,1]. conv: [0,0,h0,h1]. slice 1:3 => [0,h0]. grad [g1, -h0]. Good.

For local c_shift, c_shift = corr_counts[::-1]; positive lag s=+1 at index off+1=2. The local delta added cross +d^2 at off+j-i for move source->target? In focused, i=target, j=source, s=i-j? For move source a to target b, i=b,j=a, i-j=s? if b>a positive. Cross at off + (i-j)=off+s +d^2 and off+(j-i)=off-s +d^2. Good. The max lag c_shift at p=off+s gets correct positive delta? For term source->target, move h_b+=d,h_a-=d: change -d(1-h_b) - d h_a +d^2. Our delta for p=off+s: cross +d^2 plus contributions? Earlier for i=b,j=a, p=off+i-j=off+s: delta from i first? Let's trust formula.

Now, possible issue with local delta when i,j order and om/h full: For i=b and j=a, slices:
- i first: +d om over q all, includes q=a? source; for p=off+s? q=i? Wait first term source? Need exact for term source a, target b: p=off+s. In our formula, p=off+s corresponds i? For move d at i (target) and -d at j (source). The term source-target has first source j, second target i. The affected p is off+(i-j). def optimize_start(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h0.copy(),n)
    c0=corr_counts(h)
    best_c=c5_from_counts? just compute.
    best_h=h.copy()
    current_c=best_c
    alphas=[10.0,50.0,200.0,1000.0,4000.0]
    mu=10.0; lam=0.0
    for a in alphas:
       for stage in range(2):
          if time.time() >= deadline - 5: break
          maxit=lm_maxiter(n, deadline-time.time())
          if maxit < 20: break
          h2,comp=run_lm(h,n,a,lam,mu,maxit)
          c2=evaluate_c5(h2,n)
          if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
              best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
          if c2 <= best_c + 0.005:
              h=h2; current_c=c2
          # if c2 much worse, keep current h (which should be best or previous)
          if not np.isfinite(comp): comp=0.0
          lam += mu*comp
          if abs(comp) > 1e-4:
              mu = min(mu*2.0, 5e4)
          else:
              mu = min(mu*1.2, 5e4)
    # Maybe active polish on h if time, also on best_h if h worse
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       start_active = h if evaluate_c5(h,n) <= best_c + 0.003 else best_h
       ha,ca = active_polish(start_active,n,deadline-5,max_steps=250)
       if ca < best_c:
          best_c=ca; best_h=ha
    return best_h,best_c
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except Exception:
    fftconvolve = None


def corr_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def evaluate_c5(h, n):
    c = corr_counts(h)
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def safe_convolve(a, b):
    nn = max(len(a), len(b))
    if fftconvolve is not None and nn > 700:
        try:
            return np.asarray(fftconvolve(a, b, mode='full'), dtype=float)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return np.convolve(a, b, mode='full')


def finalize_sum(x, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(6):
        s = float(np.sum(x))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = x < 1.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                x[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            mask = x > 0.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                x[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def project_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    if v.size != n:
        v = np.resize(v, n)
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    vmin = float(np.min(v))
    lo = -vmax - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - vmin
    for _ in range(64):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(v + tau, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau
    x = np.clip(v + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return finalize_sum(x, n)


def start_key(item):
    return (float(item[0]), int(item[1]))


def smooth_once(x, n, alpha):
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        alpha = 1.0
    c = corr_counts(x)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = alpha * (c - m)
    ex = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if (not math.isfinite(s)) or s <= 0.0:
        W = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
        W[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        W = ex / s

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    f = (m + math.log(s) / alpha) * dx

    off = n - 1
    g = 1.0 - x
    c1 = safe_convolve(g, W)
    c2 = safe_convolve(x, W[::-1])
    grad = (c1[off:off + n] - c2[off:off + n]) * dx

    if not math.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e30
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    return float(f), grad


def alm_fun(x, n, alpha, lam, mu):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    f, grad = smooth_once(x, n, alpha)
    if not math.isfinite(f) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    comp = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
    val = f + lam * comp + 0.5 * mu * comp * comp
    if (not math.isfinite(val)) or val > 1e25:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if lam != 0.0 or mu != 0.0:
        grad = grad + (lam + mu * comp)
    return float(val), grad


def run_lm(h0, n, alpha, lam, mu, maxiter):
    if maxiter <= 0:
        return h0.copy(), 0.0
    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
        opts = {'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'maxls': 30}
        res = minimize(alm_fun, h0, args=(n, alpha, lam, mu),
                       method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=opts)
        x = getattr(res, 'x', None)
        if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return h0.copy(), 0.0
        comp = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
        if not math.isfinite(comp):
            comp = 0.0
        hp = project_feasible(x, n)
        return hp, comp
    except Exception:
        return h0.copy(), 0.0


def lm_maxiter(n, time_left):
    if time_left < 25.0:
        return 0
    if n <= 300:
        base = 140
    elif n <= 500:
        base = 90
    elif n <= 700:
        base = 60
    else:
        base = 40
    if time_left < 60.0:
        base = max(15, int(base * 0.5))
    if time_left < 35.0:
        base = max(10, int(base * 0.5))
    return base


def single_lag_grad(h, n, p):
    off = n - 1
    s = off - int(p)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if s >= 0:
        m = n - s
        if m <= 0:
            return grad
        a = np.arange(m)
        grad[a] += 1.0 - h[a + s]
        grad[a + s] -= h[a]
    else:
        q = -s
        m = n - q
        if m <= 0:
            return grad
        a = np.arange(q, n)
        grad[a] += 1.0 - h[a - q]
        grad[a - q] -= h[a]
    return grad


def active_polish(h, n, deadline, max_steps=250):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    c = corr_counts(h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    best_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_c = best_m * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    off = n - 1
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_steps)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        top_k = 4 if n > 400 else 5
        active = np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]
        maxc = float(np.max(c[active]))
        w = np.exp(40.0 * (c[active] - maxc))
        ws = float(np.sum(w))
        if ws <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ws):
            w = np.ones_like(w, dtype=float) / float(len(w))
        else:
            w = w / ws

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for idx, wt in zip(active, w):
            if float(wt) > 1e-10:
                grad += float(wt) * single_lag_grad(h, n, int(idx))

        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gn <= 1e-14:
            break

        base = 1.0 / float(n)
        accepted = False
        for mult in (5.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            hp = project_feasible(h - base * mult * grad, n)
            cn = corr_counts(hp)
            mn = float(np.max(cn))
            if mn < best_m - 1e-12:
                h = hp
                c = cn
                best_m = mn
                best_c = mn * dx
                best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 90:
                break

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def local_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=300000,
                 T0_scale=0.003, Tend_scale=0.00005):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    c = corr_counts(h)[::-1].copy()
    off = n - 1
    L = 2 * off + 1

    cur_m = float(np.max(c))
    best_m = cur_m
    best_h = h.copy()

    temp = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    cand = np.empty(L, dtype=float)

    om = 1.0 - h
    hr = h[::-1]

    total = int(min(max_iters, max(20000, int(50000000.0 / max(1, n)))))
    T0 = max(1e-6, T0_scale * float(n))
    Tend = max(1e-8, Tend_scale * float(n))

    pmax = int(np.argmax(c))
    accept_count = 0

    for it in range(total):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        T = T0 * (Tend / T0) ** (float(it) / float(total))

        if it % 50 == 0:
            pmax = int(np.argmax(c))

        if it % 2000 == 0 and cur_m > best_m + max(0.4, 0.01 * best_m):
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = corr_counts(h)[::-1].copy()
            cur_m = float(np.max(c))
            om = 1.0 - h
            hr = h[::-1]
            pmax = int(np.argmax(c))
            continue

        i = 0
        j = 0
        d = 0.0

        if rng.random() < 0.70 and pmax != off:
            s = int(pmax - off)
            if s >= 0:
                a_start = 0
                a_stop = n - s
            else:
                a_start = -s
                a_stop = n
            if a_stop > a_start:
                a = int(rng.randint(a_start, a_stop))
                b = a + s
                i = int(b)
                j = int(a)
                d = float(rng.uniform(1e-4, 0.02))
            else:
                i = int(rng.randint(n))
                j = int(rng.randint(n))
                if i == j:
                    continue
                d = float(rng.uniform(1e-4, 0.05))
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    d = -d
        else:
            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            d = float(rng.uniform(1e-4, 0.05))
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                d = -d

        hi = h[i] + d
        hj = h[j] - d
        if hi < 0.0 or hi > 1.0 or hj < 0.0 or hj > 1.0:
            continue

        di = int(i)
        dj = int(j)

        temp.fill(0.0)
        temp[off - di:off + n - di] += d * om
        temp[di:di + n] += -d * hr
        temp[off - dj:off + n - dj] -= d * om
        temp[dj:dj + n] += d * hr
        temp[off] += -2.0 * d * d
        if di != dj:
            temp[off + dj - di] += d * d
            temp[off + di - dj] += d * d

        np.add(c, temp, out=cand)
        new_m = float(np.max(cand))
        diff = new_m - cur_m

        accept = False
        if new_m < cur_m - 1e-14:
            accept = True
        elif new_m < best_m - 1e-13:
            accept = True
        elif abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                accept = True
        else:
            if T > 1e-12:
                rr = rng.random()
                if rr > 1e-300 and math.log(rr) < -diff / T:
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            h[di] = hi
            h[dj] = hj
            c += temp
            cur_m = new_m
            om[di] = 1.0 - hi
            om[dj] = 1.0 - hj
            accept_count += 1

            if new_m < best_m - 1e-13:
                best_m = new_m
                best_h = h.copy()

            if accept_count % 5000 == 0:
                c = corr_counts(h)[::-1].copy()
                cur_m = float(np.max(c))
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-13:
                    best_m = cur_m
                    best_h = h.copy()
                om = 1.0 - h
                hr = h[::-1]
                pmax = int(np.argmax(c))

    h_best = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c_best = evaluate_c5(h_best, n)

    h_cur = project_feasible(h, n)
    c_cur = evaluate_c5(h_cur, n)

    if c_cur < c_best:
        return h_cur, float(c_cur)
    return h_best, float(c_best)


def optimize_start(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h0.copy(), n)
    current_c = evaluate_c5(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = current_c

    alphas = (10.0, 50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 4000.0)
    mu = 10.0
    lam = 0.0

    for a in alphas:
        for _ in range(2):
            if time.time() > deadline - 8.0:
                break
            ml = lm_maxiter(n, deadline - time.time())
            if ml <= 0:
                break

            h2, comp = run_lm(h, n, a, lam, mu, ml)
            c2 = evaluate_c5(h2, n)

            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()

            if c2 <= best_c + 0.005:
                h = h2
                current_c = c2

            if math.isfinite(comp):
                lam += mu * comp
                if abs(comp) > 1e-4:
                    mu = min(mu * 2.0, 5e4)
                else:
                    mu = min(mu * 1.25, 5e4)
                if abs(lam) > 1e3:
                    lam = 0.0

    if time.time() < deadline - 12.0:
        active_start = h if current_c <= best_c + 0.004 else best_h
        ha, ca = active_polish(active_start, n, deadline - 4.0, max_steps=220)
        if ca < best_c:
            best_c = ca
            best_h = ha

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def build_parent_candidates(parent, deadline):
    res = []
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
    m = int(len(parent))
    if m <= 1:
        return res

    base = m if (m % 2 == 0) else m + 1
    if base < 120:
        base = 120
    if base % 2:
        base += 1

    deltas = (0, -20, 20, -40, 40, 80, -80, 150, 250, 350, 500)
    ns = []
    for d in deltas:
        n = base + d
        if 120 <= n <= 800 and n % 2 == 0 and n not in ns:
            ns.append(n)

    for factor in (1.25, 1.5, 2.0):
        n = int(round(base * factor))
        if n % 2:
            n += 1
        if n < 120:
            n = 120
        if n > 800:
            n = 800
        if n % 2:
            n -= 1
        if n not in ns:
            ns.append(n)

    x_old = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(m))
    used = []

    for n in ns:
        if n in used:
            continue
        used.append(n)
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if n == m:
            hv = parent.copy()
        else:
            x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
            hv = np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent)
        hv = project_feasible(hv, n)
        c = evaluate_c5(hv, n)
        if math.isfinite(c):
            res.append((float(c), int(n), hv))

    if not res:
        hv = project_feasible(np.full(base, 0.5, dtype=float), base)
        res.append((evaluate_c5(hv, base), int(base), hv))

    return res


def select_unique_starts(starts, K):
    selected = []
    seen = []
    for item in starts:
        n = int(item[1])
        if n in seen:
            continue
        seen.append(n)
        selected.append(item)
        if len(selected) >= int(K):
            break
    return selected


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 1100.0
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    starts = []
    cand_deadline = t0 + min(80.0, max(20.0, budget_s * 0.08))
    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        starts = build_parent_candidates(parent, cand_deadline)

    if not starts:
        for n in (200, 300, 500):
            if n % 2:
                n += 1
            hv = project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float), n)
            c = evaluate_c5(hv, n)
            starts.append((float(c), int(n), hv))

    starts.sort(key=start_key)
    selected = select_unique_starts(starts, 3)

    if not selected:
        n = 250
        hv = project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float), n)
        c = evaluate_c5(hv, n)
        selected = [(float(c), int(n), hv)]

    best_h = selected[0][2].copy()
    best_n = int(selected[0][1])
    best_c = float(selected[0][0])

    if budget_s > 600.0:
        reserve_local = max(140.0, min(380.0, budget_s * 0.30))
    else:
        reserve_local = max(35.0, budget_s * 0.25)

    opt_deadline = deadline - reserve_local
    if opt_deadline < t0 + 35.0:
        opt_deadline = deadline - 20.0

    fracs = (0.5, 0.3, 0.2)
    for idx, item in enumerate(selected):
        if time.time() > opt_deadline:
            break
        _, n, h = item
        frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.1
        remaining = max(0.0, opt_deadline - time.time())
        alloc = remaining * float(frac)
        if alloc < 15.0:
            alloc = max(0.0, remaining * 0.2)
        alloc_deadline = time.time() + alloc
        if alloc_deadline > opt_deadline:
            alloc_deadline = opt_deadline
        if time.time() >= alloc_deadline:
            continue
        h_opt, c_opt = optimize_start(h, n, alloc_deadline, rng)
        if c_opt < best_c:
            best_c = float(c_opt)
            best_h = h_opt.copy()
            best_n = int(n)

    if (time.time() < opt_deadline - 80.0 and best_c > 0.38081
            and best_h is not None):
        scale = 0.02
        hp = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, len(best_h))
        hp = project_feasible(hp, best_n)
        extra_deadline = min(opt_deadline, time.time() + 110.0)
        if extra_deadline > time.time() + 20.0:
            h_opt, c_opt = optimize_start(hp, best_n, extra_deadline, rng)
            if c_opt < best_c:
                best_c = float(c_opt)
                best_h = h_opt.copy()
                best_n = int(best_n)

    local_deadline = deadline - 3.0
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < local_deadline - 8.0:
        h_l, c_l = local_search(best_h, best_n, local_deadline - 2.0, rng,
                                max_iters=300000,
                                T0_scale=0.003, Tend_scale=0.00005)
        if c_l < best_c:
            best_c = float(c_l)
            best_h = h_l.copy()
            best_n = int(len(h_l))

        if len(selected) > 1 and time.time() < local_deadline - 70.0:
            c2, n2, h2 = selected[1]
            if c2 < best_c + 0.003:
                h2_l, c2_l = local_search(h2, n2, local_deadline - 35.0, rng,
                                          max_iters=150000,
                                          T0_scale=0.002, Tend_scale=0.00003)
                if c2_l < best_c:
                    best_c = float(c2_l)
                    best_h = h2_l.copy()
                    best_n = int(n2)

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 28.0:
        ha, ca = active_polish(best_h, best_n, deadline - 15.0, max_steps=120)
        if ca < best_c:
            best_c = float(ca)
            best_h = ha
            best_n = int(len(best_h))

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        h_l, c_l = local_search(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2.0, rng,
                                max_iters=60000,
                                T0_scale=0.0006, Tend_scale=1e-5)
        if c_l < best_c:
            best_c = float(c_l)
            best_h = h_l.copy()
            best_n = int(len(h_l))

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 250
        best_h = project_feasible(np.full(best_n, 0.5, dtype=float), best_n)
        best_c = evaluate_c5(best_h, best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = evaluate_c5(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(final_c), int(best_n)
```
